```
    23→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensesImmediately verifies that PostCommit on
    24→// an ACTIVE session condenses immediately and stays ACTIVE.
    25→// With the 1:1 checkpoint model, each commit gets its own checkpoint right away.
    26→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensesImmediately(t *testing.T) {
    27→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    28→	t.Chdir(dir)
    29→
    30→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    31→	require.NoError(t, err)
    32→
    33→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    34→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    35→
    36→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    37→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    38→
    39→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    40→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    41→	require.NoError(t, err)
    42→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    43→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    44→
    45→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch
    46→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
    47→
    48→	// Run PostCommit
    49→	err = s.PostCommit()
    50→	require.NoError(t, err)
    51→
    52→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE (immediate condensation, no deferred phase)
    53→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    54→	require.NoError(t, err)
    55→	require.NotNil(t, state)
    56→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
    57→		"ACTIVE session should stay ACTIVE after immediate condensation on GitCommit")
    58→
    59→	// Verify condensation happened: the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist
    60→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
    61→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after immediate condensation")
    62→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
    63→
    64→	// Verify StepCount was reset to 0 by condensation
    65→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
    66→		"StepCount should be reset after immediate condensation")
    67→}

    69→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses verifies that PostCommit on an IDLE
    70→// session condenses session data and cleans up the shadow branch.
    71→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses(t *testing.T) {

   114→// TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition verifies that PostCommit
   115→// is a no-op during rebase operations, leaving the session phase unchanged.
   116→func TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition(t *testing.T) {
   117→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   118→	t.Chdir(dir)
   119→
   120→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   121→	require.NoError(t, err)
   122→
   123→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   124→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-rebase"
   125→
   126→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   127→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   128→
   129→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   130→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   131→	require.NoError(t, err)
   132→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   133→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   134→
   135→	// Capture shadow branch name BEFORE any state changes
   136→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   137→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   138→
   139→	// Simulate rebase in progress by creating .git/rebase-merge/ directory
   140→	gitDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".git")
   141→	rebaseMergeDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")
   142→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(rebaseMergeDir, 0o755))
   143→	defer os.RemoveAll(rebaseMergeDir)
   144→
   145→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   146→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b2")
   147→
   148→	// Run PostCommit
   149→	err = s.PostCommit()
   150→	require.NoError(t, err)
   151→
   152→	// Verify phase stayed ACTIVE (no transition during rebase)
   153→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   154→	require.NoError(t, err)
   155→	require.NotNil(t, state)
   156→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   157→		"session should stay ACTIVE during rebase (no transition)")
   158→
   159→	// Verify StepCount was NOT reset (no condensation happened)
   160→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   161→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation during rebase")
   162→
   163→	// Verify NO condensation happened (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should not exist)
   164→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   165→	require.Error(t, err,
   166→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist - no condensation during rebase")
   167→
   168→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (not cleaned up during rebase)
   169→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   170→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   171→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   172→		"shadow branch should be preserved during rebase")
   173→}
   174→
   175→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSessionAlwaysCondenses verifies that an ACTIVE session
   176→// is always condensed on GitCommit, even when it has no checkpoints or tracked files.
   177→// This is because PrepareCommitMsg already validated the trailer, so PostCommit
   178→// trusts that decision rather than re-validating via transcript analysis (which is
   179→// unreliable when subagents are still running).
   180→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSessionAlwaysCondenses(t *testing.T) {
   181→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   182→	t.Chdir(dir)
   183→
   184→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   185→	require.NoError(t, err)
   186→
   187→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   188→	idleSessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-multi"
   189→	activeSessionID := "test-postcommit-active-multi"
   190→
   191→	// Initialize the idle session with a checkpoint
   192→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, idleSessionID)
   193→
   194→	// Get worktree path and base commit from the idle session
   195→	idleState, err := s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   196→	require.NoError(t, err)
   197→	worktreePath := idleState.WorktreePath
   198→	baseCommit := idleState.BaseCommit
   199→	worktreeID := idleState.WorktreeID
   200→
   201→	// Set idle session to IDLE phase
   202→	idleState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   203→	idleState.LastInteractionTime = nil
   204→	idleState.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   205→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(idleState))
   206→
   207→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent session).
   208→	// This session is ACTIVE but has NO checkpoints (StepCount=0, no shadow branch content).
   209→	// Despite having no content, it WILL be condensed because ACTIVE sessions always
   210→	// condense — PrepareCommitMsg already validated the trailer.
   211→	now := time.Now()
   212→	activeState := &SessionState{
   213→		SessionID:           activeSessionID,
   214→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   215→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   216→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   217→		StartedAt:           now,
   218→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   219→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   220→		StepCount:           0,
   221→	}
   222→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(activeState))
   223→
   224→	// Record shadow branch name before PostCommit
   225→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(baseCommit, worktreeID)
   226→
   227→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   228→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   229→
   230→	// Run PostCommit
   231→	err = s.PostCommit()
   232→	require.NoError(t, err)
   233→
   234→	// Verify the ACTIVE session stays ACTIVE (immediate condensation model)
   235→	activeState, err = s.loadSessionState(activeSessionID)
   236→	require.NoError(t, err)
   237→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, activeState.Phase,
   238→		"ACTIVE session should stay ACTIVE after GitCommit")
   239→
   240→	// Verify both sessions condensed (entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist)
   241→	idleState, err = s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   242→	require.NoError(t, err)
   243→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   244→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   245→	require.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   246→
   247→	// Verify IDLE session's StepCount was reset by condensation
   248→	assert.Equal(t, 0, idleState.StepCount,
   249→		"IDLE session StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   250→
   251→	// Verify shadow branch is cleaned up because ALL sessions condensed
   252→	// (both IDLE and ACTIVE were condensed on this commit)
   253→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   254→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   255→	assert.Error(t, err,
   256→		"shadow branch should be deleted when all sessions have been condensed")
   257→}
   258→
   259→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
   260→// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
   261→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   262→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   263→	t.Chdir(dir)
   264→
   265→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   266→	require.NoError(t, err)
   267→
   268→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   269→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-idle"
   270→
   271→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   272→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   273→
   274→	// Set phase to IDLE
   275→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   276→	require.NoError(t, err)
   277→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   278→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   279→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   280→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   281→
   282→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
   283→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   284→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   285→
   286→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   287→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   288→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   289→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   290→
   291→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   292→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   293→
   294→	// Run PostCommit
   295→	err = s.PostCommit()
   296→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   297→
   298→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   299→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   300→	require.NoError(t, err)
   301→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   302→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   303→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   304→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   305→
   306→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   307→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   308→	require.Error(t, err,
   309→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   310→
   311→	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails
   312→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   313→		"phase should remain IDLE when condensation fails")
   314→}
   315→
   316→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_PreservesBaseCommit verifies that when
   317→// an IDLE session has no new transcript content since last condensation,
   318→// PostCommit skips condensation and does NOT update BaseCommit.
   319→//
   320→// This prevents the bug where old IDLE sessions would have their BaseCommit
   321→// incorrectly updated, causing them to be condensed on future commits.
   322→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_PreservesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   323→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   324→	t.Chdir(dir)
   325→
   326→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   327→	require.NoError(t, err)
   328→
   329→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   330→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-no-content"
   331→
   332→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   333→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   334→
   335→	// Set phase to IDLE with CheckpointTranscriptStart matching transcript length (2 lines)
   336→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   337→	require.NoError(t, err)
   338→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   339→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   340→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   341→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   342→
   343→	// Record shadow branch name and original BaseCommit
   344→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   345→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   346→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   347→
   348→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   349→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "f6a1b2c3d4e5")
   350→
   351→	// Run PostCommit
   352→	err = s.PostCommit()
   353→	require.NoError(t, err)
   354→
   355→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (IDLE sessions don't get BaseCommit updated)
   356→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   357→	require.NoError(t, err)
   358→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   359→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated for IDLE session with no new content")
   360→
   361→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not deleted, no condensation)
   362→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   363→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   364→	require.NoError(t, err,
   365→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   366→
   367→	// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   368→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   369→	require.Error(t, err,
   370→		"entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should NOT exist when no condensation happened")
   371→
   372→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   373→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   374→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   375→}

   377→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
   378→// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
   379→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
   380→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   381→	t.Chdir(dir)
   382→
   383→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   384→	require.NoError(t, err)
   385→
   386→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   387→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-condenses"
   388→
   389→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   390→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   391→
   392→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   393→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   394→	require.NoError(t, err)
   395→	now := time.Now()
   396→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   397→	state.EndedAt = &now
   398→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   399→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   400→
   401→	// Record shadow branch name
   402→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   403→
   404→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   405→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
   406→
   407→	// Run PostCommit
   408→	err = s.PostCommit()
   409→	require.NoError(t, err)
   410→
   411→	// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists (condensation happened)
   412→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   413→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 branch should exist after condensation")
   414→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   415→
   416→	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
   417→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   418→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   419→	require.Error(t, err,
   420→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
   421→
   422→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   423→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   424→	require.NoError(t, err)
   425→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   426→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   427→
   428→	// Phase stays ENDED
   429→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   430→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED after condensation")
   431→}

   433→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
   434→// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
   435→// and does NOT update BaseCommit.

   460→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))

   484→

   494→}

   613→// TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions verifies that HandleTurnEnd with no actions
   614→// is a no-op (normal ACTIVE → IDLE transition has no strategy-specific actions).
   615→func TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions(t *testing.T) {
   616→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   617→	t.Chdir(dir)
   618→
   619→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   620→	require.NoError(t, err)
   621→
   622→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   623→	sessionID := "test-turnend-no-actions"
   624→
   625→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   626→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   627→
   628→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (normal turn, no commit during turn)
   629→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   630→	require.NoError(t, err)
   631→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   632→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   633→
   634→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   635→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   636→
   637→	// ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE with no strategy-specific actions
   638→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   639→
   640→	// Apply transition with no-op handler (no strategy actions for ACTIVE → IDLE)
   641→	err = session.ApplyTransition(state, result, session.NoOpActionHandler{})
   642→	require.NoError(t, err)
   643→
   644→	// Call HandleTurnEnd — should be a no-op (no TurnCheckpointIDs)
   645→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state)
   646→	require.NoError(t, err)
   647→
   648→	// Verify state is unchanged
   649→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   650→		"BaseCommit should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
   651→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   652→		"StepCount should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
   653→
   654→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up)
   655→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   656→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   657→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   658→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   659→		"shadow branch should still exist after no-op turn end")
   660→}
   661→
   662→// TestPostCommit_FilesTouched_ResetsAfterCondensation verifies that FilesTouched
   663→// is reset after condensation, so subsequent condensations only contain the files
   664→// touched since the last commit — not the accumulated history.
   665→func TestPostCommit_FilesTouched_ResetsAfterCondensation(t *testing.T) {
   666→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   667→	t.Chdir(dir)
   668→
   669→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   670→	require.NoError(t, err)
   671→
   672→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   673→	sessionID := "test-filestouched-reset"
   674→
   675→	// --- Round 1: Save checkpoint touching files A.txt and B.txt ---
   676→
   677→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   678→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   679→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   680→
   681→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"round 1 prompt"}}
   682→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"round 1 response"}}
   683→`
   684→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
   685→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   686→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
   687→
   688→	// Create files A.txt and B.txt
   689→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "A.txt"), []byte("file A"), 0o644))
   690→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "B.txt"), []byte("file B"), 0o644))
   691→
   692→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   693→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   694→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   695→		NewFiles:       []string{"A.txt", "B.txt"},
   696→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   697→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   698→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   699→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1: files A and B",
   700→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   701→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   702→	})
   703→	require.NoError(t, err)
   704→
   705→	// Set phase to IDLE so PostCommit triggers immediate condensation
   706→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   707→	require.NoError(t, err)
   708→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   709→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   710→
   711→	// Verify FilesTouched has A.txt and B.txt before condensation
   712→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"A.txt", "B.txt"}, state.FilesTouched,
   713→		"FilesTouched should contain A.txt and B.txt before first condensation")
   714→
   715→	// --- Commit A.txt, B.txt and condense (round 1) ---
   716→	checkpointID1 := "a1a2a3a4a5a6"
   717→	commitFilesWithTrailer(t, repo, dir, checkpointID1, "A.txt", "B.txt")
   718→
   719→	err = s.PostCommit()
   720→	require.NoError(t, err)
   721→
   722→	// Verify condensation happened
   723→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   724→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/checkpoints/v1 should exist after first condensation")
   725→
   726→	// Verify first condensation contains A.txt and B.txt
   727→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   728→	cpID1 := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointID1)
   729→	summary1, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), cpID1)
   730→	require.NoError(t, err)
   731→	require.NotNil(t, summary1)
   732→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"A.txt", "B.txt"}, summary1.FilesTouched,
   733→		"First condensation should contain A.txt and B.txt")
   734→
   735→	// Verify FilesTouched was reset after condensation
   736→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   737→	require.NoError(t, err)
   738→	assert.Nil(t, state.FilesTouched,
   739→		"FilesTouched should be nil after condensation (all files were committed)")
   740→
   741→	// --- Round 2: Save checkpoint touching files C.txt and D.txt ---
   742→
   743→	// Append to transcript for round 2
   744→	transcript2 := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"round 2 prompt"}}
   745→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"round 2 response"}}
   746→`
   747→	f, err := os.OpenFile(
   748→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   749→		os.O_APPEND|os.O_WRONLY, 0o644)
   750→	require.NoError(t, err)
   751→	_, err = f.WriteString(transcript2)
   752→	require.NoError(t, err)
   753→	require.NoError(t, f.Close())
   754→
   755→	// Create files C.txt and D.txt
   756→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "C.txt"), []byte("file C"), 0o644))
   757→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "D.txt"), []byte("file D"), 0o644))
   758→
   759→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   760→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   761→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   762→		NewFiles:       []string{"C.txt", "D.txt"},
   763→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   764→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   765→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   766→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 2: files C and D",
   767→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   768→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   769→	})
   770→	require.NoError(t, err)
   771→
   772→	// Set phase to IDLE for immediate condensation
   773→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   774→	require.NoError(t, err)
   775→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   776→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   777→
   778→	// Verify FilesTouched only has C.txt and D.txt (NOT A.txt, B.txt)
   779→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"C.txt", "D.txt"}, state.FilesTouched,
   780→		"FilesTouched should only contain C.txt and D.txt after reset")
   781→
   782→	// --- Commit C.txt, D.txt and condense (round 2) ---
   783→	checkpointID2 := "b1b2b3b4b5b6"
   784→	commitFilesWithTrailer(t, repo, dir, checkpointID2, "C.txt", "D.txt")
   785→
   786→	err = s.PostCommit()
   787→	require.NoError(t, err)
   788→
   789→	// Verify second condensation contains ONLY C.txt and D.txt
   790→	cpID2 := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointID2)
   791→	summary2, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), cpID2)
   792→	require.NoError(t, err)
   793→	require.NotNil(t, summary2, "Second condensation should exist")
   794→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"C.txt", "D.txt"}, summary2.FilesTouched,
   795→		"Second condensation should only contain C.txt and D.txt, not accumulated files from first condensation")
   796→}
   797→
   798→// TestSubtractFiles verifies that subtractFiles correctly removes files present
   799→// in the exclude set and preserves files not in it.
   800→func TestSubtractFiles(t *testing.T) {
   801→	t.Parallel()
   802→
   803→	tests := []struct {
   804→		name     string
   805→		files    []string
   806→		exclude  map[string]struct{}
   807→		expected []string
   808→	}{
   809→		{
   810→			name:     "no overlap",
   811→			files:    []string{"a.txt", "b.txt"},
   812→			exclude:  map[string]struct{}{"c.txt": {}},
   813→			expected: []string{"a.txt", "b.txt"},
   814→		},
   815→		{
   816→			name:     "full overlap",
   817→			files:    []string{"a.txt", "b.txt"},
   818→			exclude:  map[string]struct{}{"a.txt": {}, "b.txt": {}},
   819→			expected: nil,
   820→		},
   821→		{
   822→			name:     "partial overlap",
   823→			files:    []string{"a.txt", "b.txt", "c.txt"},
   824→			exclude:  map[string]struct{}{"b.txt": {}},
   825→			expected: []string{"a.txt", "c.txt"},
   826→		},
   827→		{
   828→			name:     "empty files",
   829→			files:    []string{},
   830→			exclude:  map[string]struct{}{"a.txt": {}},
   831→			expected: nil,
   832→		},
   833→		{
   834→			name:     "empty exclude",
   835→			files:    []string{"a.txt", "b.txt"},
   836→			exclude:  map[string]struct{}{},
   837→			expected: []string{"a.txt", "b.txt"},
   838→		},
   839→	}
   840→
   841→	for _, tt := range tests {
   842→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   843→			t.Parallel()
   844→			result := subtractFiles(tt.files, tt.exclude)
   845→			assert.Equal(t, tt.expected, result)
   846→		})
   847→	}
   848→}
   849→
   850→// TestFilesChangedInCommit verifies that filesChangedInCommit correctly extracts
   851→// the set of files changed in a commit by diffing against its parent.
   852→func TestFilesChangedInCommit(t *testing.T) {
   853→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   854→	t.Chdir(dir)
   855→
   856→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   857→	require.NoError(t, err)
   858→
   859→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   860→	require.NoError(t, err)
   861→
   862→	// Create files and commit them
   863→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "file1.txt"), []byte("content1"), 0o644))
   864→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "file2.txt"), []byte("content2"), 0o644))
   865→	_, err = wt.Add("file1.txt")
   866→	require.NoError(t, err)
   867→	_, err = wt.Add("file2.txt")
   868→	require.NoError(t, err)
   869→
   870→	commitHash, err := wt.Commit("add files", &git.CommitOptions{
   871→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
   872→	})
   873→	require.NoError(t, err)
   874→
   875→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   876→	require.NoError(t, err)
   877→
   878→	changed := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   879→	assert.Contains(t, changed, "file1.txt")
   880→	assert.Contains(t, changed, "file2.txt")
   881→	// test.txt was in the initial commit, not this one
   882→	assert.NotContains(t, changed, "test.txt")
   883→}
   884→
   885→// TestFilesChangedInCommit_InitialCommit verifies that filesChangedInCommit
   886→// handles the initial commit (no parent) by listing all files.
   887→func TestFilesChangedInCommit_InitialCommit(t *testing.T) {
   888→	dir := t.TempDir()
   889→	t.Chdir(dir)
   890→
   891→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(dir, false)
   892→	require.NoError(t, err)
   893→
   894→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   895→	require.NoError(t, err)
   896→	cfg.User.Name = "Test"
   897→	cfg.User.Email = "test@test.com"
   898→	require.NoError(t, repo.SetConfig(cfg))
   899→
   900→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   901→	require.NoError(t, err)
   902→
   903→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "init.txt"), []byte("initial"), 0o644))
   904→	_, err = wt.Add("init.txt")
   905→	require.NoError(t, err)
   906→
   907→	commitHash, err := wt.Commit("initial", &git.CommitOptions{
   908→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
   909→	})
   910→	require.NoError(t, err)
   911→
   912→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   913→	require.NoError(t, err)
   914→
   915→	changed := filesChangedInCommit(commit)
   916→	assert.Contains(t, changed, "init.txt")
   917→	assert.Len(t, changed, 1)
   918→}

   920→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarryForward_PartialCommit verifies that when an
   921→// ACTIVE session has touched files A, B, C but only A and B are committed, the
   922→// remaining file C is carried forward to a new shadow branch.
   923→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarryForward_PartialCommit(t *testing.T) {
   924→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   925→	t.Chdir(dir)
   926→
   927→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   928→	require.NoError(t, err)
   929→
   930→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   931→	sessionID := "test-carry-forward-partial"
   932→
   933→	// Create metadata directory with transcript
   934→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   935→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   936→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   937→
   938→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"create files A B C"}}
   939→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"creating files"}}
   940→`
   941→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
   942→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   943→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
   944→
   945→	// Create all three files
   946→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "A.txt"), []byte("file A"), 0o644))
   947→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "B.txt"), []byte("file B"), 0o644))
   948→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "C.txt"), []byte("file C"), 0o644))
   949→
   950→	// Save checkpoint with all three files
   951→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   952→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   953→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   954→		NewFiles:       []string{"A.txt", "B.txt", "C.txt"},
   955→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   956→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   957→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   958→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint: files A, B, C",
   959→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   960→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   961→	})
   962→	require.NoError(t, err)
   963→
   964→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (agent mid-turn)
   965→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   966→	require.NoError(t, err)
   967→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   968→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   969→
   970→	// Verify FilesTouched contains all three files
   971→	assert.ElementsMatch(t, []string{"A.txt", "B.txt", "C.txt"}, state.FilesTouched)
   972→
   973→	// Commit ONLY A.txt and B.txt (not C.txt) with checkpoint trailer
   974→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   975→	require.NoError(t, err)
   976→	_, err = wt.Add("A.txt")
   977→	require.NoError(t, err)
   978→	_, err = wt.Add("B.txt")
   979→	require.NoError(t, err)
   980→
   981→	cpID := "cf1cf2cf3cf4"
   982→	commitMsg := "commit A and B\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID + "\n"
   983→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   984→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
   985→	})
   986→	require.NoError(t, err)
   987→
   988→	// Run PostCommit
   989→	err = s.PostCommit()
   990→	require.NoError(t, err)
   991→
   992→	// Verify session stayed ACTIVE
   993→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   994→	require.NoError(t, err)
   995→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
   996→
   997→	// Verify carry-forward: FilesTouched should now only contain C.txt
   998→	assert.Equal(t, []string{"C.txt"}, state.FilesTouched,
   999→		"carry-forward should preserve only the uncommitted file C.txt")
  1000→
  1001→	// Verify StepCount was set to 1 (carry-forward creates a new checkpoint)
  1002→	assert.Equal(t, 1, state.StepCount,
  1003→		"carry-forward should set StepCount to 1")
  1004→
  1005→	// Verify CheckpointTranscriptStart was reset to 0 (prompt-level carry-forward)
  1006→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
  1007→		"carry-forward should reset CheckpointTranscriptStart to 0 for full transcript reprocessing")
  1008→
  1009→	// Verify LastCheckpointID was cleared (next commit generates fresh ID)
  1010→	assert.Empty(t, state.LastCheckpointID,
  1011→		"carry-forward should clear LastCheckpointID")
  1012→
  1013→	// Verify a new shadow branch exists at the new HEAD
  1014→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1015→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch), true)
  1016→	assert.NoError(t, err,
  1017→		"carry-forward should create a new shadow branch at the new HEAD")
  1018→}
  1019→
  1020→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarryForward_AllCommitted verifies that when an
  1021→// ACTIVE session's files are ALL included in the commit, no carry-forward occurs.
  1022→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CarryForward_AllCommitted(t *testing.T) {
  1023→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1024→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1025→
  1026→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1027→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1028→
  1029→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1030→	sessionID := "test-carry-forward-all"
  1031→
  1032→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint with files A and B
  1033→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
  1034→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
  1035→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
  1036→
  1037→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"create files A B"}}
  1038→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"creating files"}}
  1039→`
  1040→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
  1041→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
  1042→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
  1043→
  1044→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "A.txt"), []byte("file A"), 0o644))
  1045→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "B.txt"), []byte("file B"), 0o644))
  1046→
  1047→	err = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
  1048→		SessionID:      sessionID,
  1049→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
  1050→		NewFiles:       []string{"A.txt", "B.txt"},
  1051→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
  1052→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
  1053→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
  1054→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint: files A, B",
  1055→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1056→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1057→	})
  1058→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1059→
  1060→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
  1061→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1062→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1063→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1064→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1065→
  1066→	// Commit ALL files (A.txt and B.txt) with checkpoint trailer
  1067→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1068→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1069→	_, err = wt.Add("A.txt")
  1070→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1071→	_, err = wt.Add("B.txt")
  1072→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1073→
  1074→	cpID := "cf5cf6cf7cf8"
  1075→	commitMsg := "commit A and B\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID + "\n"
  1076→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
  1077→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
  1078→	})
  1079→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1080→
  1081→	// Run PostCommit
  1082→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1083→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1084→
  1085→	// Verify session stayed ACTIVE
  1086→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1087→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1088→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase)
  1089→
  1090→	// Verify NO carry-forward: FilesTouched should be nil (all condensed, nothing remaining)
  1091→	assert.Nil(t, state.FilesTouched,
  1092→		"when all files are committed, no carry-forward should occur (FilesTouched cleared by condensation)")
  1093→
  1094→	// Verify StepCount was reset to 0 by condensation (not 1 from carry-forward)
  1095→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
  1096→		"without carry-forward, StepCount should be reset to 0 by condensation")
  1097→}

  1099→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_RecordsTurnCheckpointIDs verifies that PostCommit
  1100→// records the checkpoint ID in TurnCheckpointIDs for ACTIVE sessions.
  1101→// This enables HandleTurnEnd to finalize all checkpoints with the full transcript.
  1102→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_RecordsTurnCheckpointIDs(t *testing.T) {
  1103→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1104→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1105→
  1106→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1107→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1108→
  1109→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1110→	sessionID := "test-turn-checkpoint-ids"
  1111→
  1112→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
  1113→
  1114→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
  1115→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1116→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1117→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1118→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil // Start clean
  1119→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1120→
  1121→	// Create first commit with checkpoint trailer
  1122→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
  1123→
  1124→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1125→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1126→
  1127→	// Verify TurnCheckpointIDs was populated
  1128→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1129→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1130→	assert.Equal(t, []string{"a1b2c3d4e5f6"}, state.TurnCheckpointIDs,
  1131→		"TurnCheckpointIDs should contain the checkpoint ID after condensation")
  1132→}
  1133→
  1134→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_DoesNotRecordTurnCheckpointIDs verifies that PostCommit
  1135→// does NOT record TurnCheckpointIDs for IDLE sessions.
  1136→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_DoesNotRecordTurnCheckpointIDs(t *testing.T) {
  1137→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1138→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1139→
  1140→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1141→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1142→
  1143→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1144→	sessionID := "test-idle-no-turn-ids"
  1145→
  1146→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
  1147→
  1148→	// Set phase to IDLE with files touched so overlap check passes
  1149→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1150→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1151→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
  1152→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
  1153→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1154→
  1155→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b2")
  1156→
  1157→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1158→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1159→
  1160→	// Verify TurnCheckpointIDs was NOT set (IDLE sessions don't need finalization)
  1161→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1162→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1163→	assert.Empty(t, state.TurnCheckpointIDs,
  1164→		"TurnCheckpointIDs should not be populated for IDLE sessions")
  1165→}
  1166→
  1167→// TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure verifies that HandleTurnEnd continues
  1168→// processing remaining checkpoints when one UpdateCommitted call fails.
  1169→// This locks the best-effort behavior: valid checkpoints get finalized even
  1170→// when one checkpoint ID is invalid or missing from entire/checkpoints/v1.
  1171→func TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure(t *testing.T) {
  1172→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1173→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1174→
  1175→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1176→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1177→
  1178→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1179→	sessionID := "test-partial-failure"
  1180→
  1181→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
  1182→
  1183→	// Set phase to ACTIVE and create a transcript file with updated content
  1184→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1185→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1186→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1187→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = nil
  1188→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1189→
  1190→	// First commit → creates real checkpoint on entire/checkpoints/v1
  1191→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
  1192→	require.NoError(t, s.PostCommit())
  1193→
  1194→	// Write new content and create a second real checkpoint
  1195→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "second.txt"), []byte("second file"), 0o644))
  1196→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID) // refresh shadow branch
  1197→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1198→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1199→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1200→	// Preserve TurnCheckpointIDs from the first commit
  1201→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = []string{"a1b2c3d4e5f6"}
  1202→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1203→
  1204→	commitFilesWithTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1", "second.txt")
  1205→	require.NoError(t, s.PostCommit())
  1206→
  1207→	// Verify we now have 2 real checkpoint IDs
  1208→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
  1209→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1210→	require.Len(t, state.TurnCheckpointIDs, 2,
  1211→		"Should have 2 real checkpoint IDs after 2 mid-turn commits")
  1212→
  1213→	// Inject a fake 3rd checkpoint ID that doesn't exist on entire/checkpoints/v1
  1214→	state.TurnCheckpointIDs = append(state.TurnCheckpointIDs, "ffffffffffff")
  1215→
  1216→	// Write a full transcript file for HandleTurnEnd to read
  1217→	fullTranscript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"build something"}}
  1218→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"done building"}}
  1219→{"type":"human","message":{"content":"now test it"}}
  1220→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"tests pass"}}
  1221→`
  1222→	transcriptPath := filepath.Join(dir, ".entire", "metadata", sessionID, "full_transcript.jsonl")
  1223→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), 0o755))
  1224→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, []byte(fullTranscript), 0o644))
  1225→	state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1226→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
  1227→
  1228→	// Call HandleTurnEnd — should NOT return error (best-effort)
  1229→	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state)
  1230→	require.NoError(t, err,
  1231→		"HandleTurnEnd should return nil even with partial failures (best-effort)")
  1232→
  1233→	// TurnCheckpointIDs should be cleared regardless of partial failure
  1234→	assert.Empty(t, state.TurnCheckpointIDs,
  1235→		"TurnCheckpointIDs should be cleared after HandleTurnEnd, even with errors")
  1236→
  1237→	// Verify the 2 valid checkpoints were finalized with the full transcript
  1238→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
  1239→	for _, cpIDStr := range []string{"a1b2c3d4e5f6", "b2c3d4e5f6a1"} {
  1240→		cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(cpIDStr)
  1241→		content, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID, 0)
  1242→		require.NoError(t, readErr,
  1243→			"Should be able to read finalized checkpoint %s", cpIDStr)
  1244→		assert.Contains(t, string(content.Transcript), "now test it",
  1245→			"Checkpoint %s should contain the full transcript (including later messages)", cpIDStr)
  1246→	}
  1247→}
  1248→
  1249→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
  1250→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
  1251→// Also modifies test.txt to "agent modified content" and includes it in the checkpoint,
  1252→// so content-aware carry-forward comparisons work correctly when commitFilesWithTrailer
  1253→// commits the same content.
  1254→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
  1255→	t.Helper()
  1256→
  1257→	// Modify test.txt with agent content (same content that commitFilesWithTrailer will commit)
  1258→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
  1259→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte("agent modified content"), 0o644))
  1260→
  1261→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
  1262→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
  1263→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
  1264→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
  1265→
  1266→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
  1267→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
  1268→`
  1269→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
  1270→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
  1271→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
  1272→
  1273→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
  1274→	// Include test.txt as a modified file so it's saved to the shadow branch
  1275→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
  1276→		SessionID:      sessionID,
  1277→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{"test.txt"},
  1278→		NewFiles:       []string{},
  1279→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
  1280→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
  1281→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
  1282→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
  1283→		AuthorName:     "Test",
  1284→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
  1285→	})
  1286→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
  1287→}
  1288→
  1289→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
  1290→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
  1291→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
  1292→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
  1293→	t.Helper()
  1294→	commitFilesWithTrailer(t, repo, dir, checkpointIDStr, "test.txt")
  1295→}
  1296→
  1297→// commitFilesWithTrailer stages the given files and commits with a checkpoint trailer.
  1298→// Files must already exist on disk. The test.txt file is modified to ensure there's always something to commit.
  1299→// Important: For tests using content-aware carry-forward, call setupSessionWithCheckpointAndFile first
  1300→// so the shadow branch has the same content that will be committed.
  1301→func commitFilesWithTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string, files ...string) {
  1302→	t.Helper()
  1303→
  1304→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
  1305→
  1306→	// Modify test.txt with agent-like content that matches what setupSessionWithCheckpointAndFile saves
  1307→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
  1308→	content := "agent modified content"
  1309→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
  1310→
  1311→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1312→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1313→
  1314→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
  1315→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1316→	for _, f := range files {
  1317→		_, err = wt.Add(f)
  1318→		require.NoError(t, err)
  1319→	}
  1320→
  1321→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
  1322→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
  1323→		Author: &object.Signature{
  1324→			Name:  "Test",
  1325→			Email: "test@test.com",
  1326→			When:  time.Now(),
  1327→		},
  1328→	})
  1329→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
  1330→}
  1331→
  1332→// TestPostCommit_OldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an IDLE
  1333→// session from a previous commit exists, and a NEW session makes a commit, the
  1334→// old IDLE session's BaseCommit is NOT updated to the new HEAD.
  1335→//
  1336→// This is a regression test for the bug where old sessions (IDLE/ENDED) would
  1337→// have their BaseCommit updated, causing them to be incorrectly condensed on
  1338→// future commits because their BaseCommit matched the new shadow branch.
  1339→func TestPostCommit_OldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
  1340→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1341→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1342→
  1343→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1344→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1345→
  1346→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1347→
  1348→	// --- Create an old IDLE session from a previous commit ---
  1349→	oldSessionID := "old-idle-session"
  1350→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, oldSessionID)
  1351→
  1352→	oldState, err := s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
  1353→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1354→	oldState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
  1355→	oldState.FilesTouched = []string{"old-file.txt"} // Has files touched (important for bug)
  1356→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(oldState))
  1357→
  1358→	// Record the old session's BaseCommit BEFORE the new commit
  1359→	oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit := oldState.BaseCommit
  1360→
  1361→	// Create a commit to move HEAD forward (simulating old session was condensed)
  1362→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1363→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1364→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "unrelated.txt"), []byte("unrelated"), 0o644))
  1365→	_, err = wt.Add("unrelated.txt")
  1366→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1367→	_, err = wt.Commit("unrelated commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
  1368→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
  1369→	})
  1370→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1371→
  1372→	// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---
  1373→	newSessionID := "new-active-session"
  1374→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, newSessionID)
  1375→
  1376→	newState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
  1377→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1378→	newState.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1379→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))
  1380→
  1381→	// --- Commit from the new session ---
  1382→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
  1383→
  1384→	// Get new HEAD for comparison
  1385→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1386→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1387→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
  1388→
  1389→	// Run PostCommit
  1390→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1391→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1392→
  1393→	// --- Verify: old IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated ---
  1394→	oldState, err = s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
  1395→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1396→	assert.Equal(t, oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit, oldState.BaseCommit,
  1397→		"OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated when a different session commits")
  1398→	assert.NotEqual(t, newHead, oldState.BaseCommit,
  1399→		"OLD IDLE session's BaseCommit should NOT match new HEAD")
  1400→
  1401→	// New ACTIVE session's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated (it was condensed)
  1402→	newState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
  1403→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1404→	assert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,
  1405→		"NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated after condensation")
  1406→}
  1407→
  1408→// TestPostCommit_OldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated verifies that when an ENDED
  1409→// session from a previous commit exists (with no new content to condense), and a
  1410→// NEW session makes a commit, the old ENDED session's BaseCommit is NOT updated.
  1411→//
  1412→// This simulates the scenario where:
  1413→// 1. Old session ran and was already condensed (no new transcript content)
  1414→// 2. Old session is now ENDED
  1415→// 3. New session commits
  1416→// 4. Old ENDED session should NOT have BaseCommit updated
  1417→func TestPostCommit_OldEndedSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated(t *testing.T) {
  1418→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
  1419→	t.Chdir(dir)
  1420→
  1421→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
  1422→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1423→
  1424→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
  1425→
  1426→	// --- Create an old ENDED session that has NO new content to condense ---
  1427→	oldSessionID := "old-ended-session"
  1428→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, oldSessionID)
  1429→
  1430→	oldState, err := s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
  1431→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1432→	now := time.Now()
  1433→	oldState.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
  1434→	oldState.EndedAt = &now
  1435→	oldState.FilesTouched = []string{"old-file.txt"} // Has files touched
  1436→	// Mark transcript as fully condensed (no new content since last checkpoint)
  1437→	// The transcript has 2 lines, so CheckpointTranscriptStart=2 means no new content
  1438→	oldState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2
  1439→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(oldState))
  1440→
  1441→	// Record the old session's BaseCommit BEFORE the new commit
  1442→	oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit := oldState.BaseCommit
  1443→
  1444→	// Create a commit to move HEAD forward
  1445→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
  1446→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1447→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "unrelated.txt"), []byte("unrelated"), 0o644))
  1448→	_, err = wt.Add("unrelated.txt")
  1449→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1450→	_, err = wt.Commit("unrelated commit without trailer", &git.CommitOptions{
  1451→		Author: &object.Signature{Name: "Test", Email: "test@test.com", When: time.Now()},
  1452→	})
  1453→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1454→
  1455→	// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---
  1456→	newSessionID := "new-active-session"
  1457→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, newSessionID)
  1458→
  1459→	newState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
  1460→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1461→	newState.Phase = session.PhaseActive
  1462→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))
  1463→
  1464→	// --- Commit from the new session ---
  1465→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b1c2d3e4f5a6")
  1466→
  1467→	// Get new HEAD for comparison
  1468→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1469→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1470→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
  1471→
  1472→	// Run PostCommit
  1473→	err = s.PostCommit()
  1474→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1475→
  1476→	// --- Verify: old ENDED session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated ---
  1477→	oldState, err = s.loadSessionState(oldSessionID)
  1478→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1479→	assert.Equal(t, oldSessionOriginalBaseCommit, oldState.BaseCommit,
  1480→		"OLD ENDED session's BaseCommit should NOT be updated when a different session commits")
  1481→	assert.NotEqual(t, newHead, oldState.BaseCommit,
  1482→		"OLD ENDED session's BaseCommit should NOT match new HEAD")
  1483→
  1484→	// New ACTIVE session's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated
  1485→	newState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)
  1486→	require.NoError(t, err)
  1487→	assert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,
  1488→		"NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated after condensation")
  1489→}
  1490→
```

```json
  "new_string": "func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {\n\t// Require that the committed files overlap with the session's files AND\n\t// have matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit would incorrectly\n\t// get this session's checkpoint.\n\t//\n\t// This applies to ALL sessions including ACTIVE ones. A stale ACTIVE session\n\t// (agent killed without Stop hook) would otherwise be condensed into every\n\t// commit because hasNew=true unconditionally for ACTIVE sessions.\n\t//\n\t// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:\n\t//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set by SaveChanges)\n\t//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions with empty FilesTouched\n\t//\n\t// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no\n\t// SaveChanges yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.\n\tshouldCondense := h.hasNew\n\tif shouldCondense && len(h.filesTouchedBefore) > 0 {\n\t\tshouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, h.filesTouchedBefore)\n\t}\n\tif shouldCondense {"
```

**Step 3: Add the regression test.** Let me first check how the existing test `TestPostCommit_OldIdleSession_BaseCommitNotUpdated` is structured so I can follow the same pattern for the stale ACTIVE session test.

Looking at the test file, I need to also check if `TestPostCommit_ActiveSessionAlwaysCondenses` (line 180) still works correctly. That test creates an ACTIVE session with StepCount=0 and no checkpoints. Since the ACTIVE session has no `FilesTouched` and no transcript path, `filesTouchedBefore` will be empty, so the overlap check will be skipped and it will trust `hasNew=true`. This should still pass as-is.

Let me also verify the first test `TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_CondensesImmediately` - it uses `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` which touches `test.txt`, then `commitWithCheckpointTrailer` commits `test.txt`. The `filesTouchedBefore` will be `["test.txt"]` (from `state.FilesTouched`), and the committed file is also `test.txt`, so the overlap check should pass. Good.

Now let me write the regression test:

```json
{
```

```json
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/2/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go",
  "old_string": "\t// New ACTIVE session's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated\n\tnewState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated after condensation\")\n}",
  "new_string": "\t// New ACTIVE session's BaseCommit SHOULD be updated\n\tnewState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"NEW ACTIVE session's BaseCommit should be updated after condensation\")\n}\n\n// TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed verifies that a stale ACTIVE\n// session (agent killed without Stop hook) is NOT condensed into an unrelated\n// commit from a different session.\n//\n// Root cause: when an agent is killed without the Stop hook firing, its session\n// remains in ACTIVE phase permanently. Previously, PostCommit unconditionally\n// set hasNew=true for ACTIVE sessions and skipped the filesOverlapWithContent\n// check, so stale ACTIVE sessions got condensed into every commit.\n//\n// The fix applies the overlap check to ALL sessions (including ACTIVE) using\n// filesTouchedBefore, so stale sessions with unrelated files are filtered out.\nfunc TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\n\t// --- Create a stale ACTIVE session from an old commit ---\n\t// This simulates an agent that was killed without the Stop hook firing.\n\tstaleSessionID := \"stale-active-session\"\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, staleSessionID)\n\n\tstaleState, err := s.loadSessionState(staleSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstaleState.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\t// The stale session touched \"test.txt\" (set by setupSessionWithCheckpoint)\n\t// but the new commit will modify a different file.\n\tstaleState.FilesTouched = []string{\"test.txt\"}\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(staleState))\n\n\tstaleOriginalBaseCommit := staleState.BaseCommit\n\tstaleOriginalStepCount := staleState.StepCount\n\n\t// Move HEAD forward with an unrelated commit (no trailer)\n\twt, err := repo.Worktree()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, \"unrelated.txt\"), []byte(\"unrelated work\"), 0o644))\n\t_, err = wt.Add(\"unrelated.txt\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t_, err = wt.Commit(\"unrelated commit\", &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@test.com\", When: time.Now()},\n\t})\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// --- Create a NEW ACTIVE session at the new HEAD ---\n\tnewSessionID := \"new-active-session\"\n\n\t// Create a new file for the new session (different from stale session's test.txt)\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, \"new-feature.txt\"), []byte(\"new feature content\"), 0o644))\n\n\tmetadataDir := \".entire/metadata/\" + newSessionID\n\tmetadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))\n\n\ttranscript := `{\"type\":\"human\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"add new feature\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"adding new feature\"}}\n`\n\trequire.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(\n\t\tfilepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),\n\t\t[]byte(transcript), 0o644))\n\n\terr = s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{\n\t\tSessionID:      newSessionID,\n\t\tModifiedFiles:  []string{},\n\t\tNewFiles:       []string{\"new-feature.txt\"},\n\t\tDeletedFiles:   []string{},\n\t\tMetadataDir:    metadataDir,\n\t\tMetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,\n\t\tCommitMessage:  \"Checkpoint: new feature\",\n\t\tAuthorName:     \"Test\",\n\t\tAuthorEmail:    \"test@test.com\",\n\t})\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tnewState, err := s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tnewState.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(newState))\n\n\t// --- Commit ONLY new-feature.txt (not test.txt) with checkpoint trailer ---\n\twt, err = repo.Worktree()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\t_, err = wt.Add(\"new-feature.txt\")\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tcpID := \"stale1stale2\"\n\tcommitMsg := \"add new feature\\n\\n\" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + \": \" + cpID + \"\\n\"\n\t_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{\n\t\tAuthor: &object.Signature{Name: \"Test\", Email: \"test@test.com\", When: time.Now()},\n\t})\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tnewHead := head.Hash().String()\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// --- Verify: stale ACTIVE session was NOT condensed ---\n\tstaleState, err = s.loadSessionState(staleSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// StepCount should be unchanged (not reset by condensation)\n\tassert.Equal(t, staleOriginalStepCount, staleState.StepCount,\n\t\t\"Stale ACTIVE session StepCount should NOT be reset (no condensation)\")\n\n\t// BaseCommit IS updated for ACTIVE sessions (updateBaseCommitIfChanged)\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHead, staleState.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"Stale ACTIVE session BaseCommit should be updated (ACTIVE sessions always get BaseCommit updated)\")\n\tassert.NotEqual(t, staleOriginalBaseCommit, staleState.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"Stale ACTIVE session BaseCommit should have changed\")\n\n\t// Phase stays ACTIVE\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, staleState.Phase,\n\t\t\"Stale ACTIVE session should remain ACTIVE\")\n\n\t// --- Verify: new ACTIVE session WAS condensed ---\n\tnewState, err = s.loadSessionState(newSessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// StepCount reset to 0 by condensation\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, newState.StepCount,\n\t\t\"New ACTIVE session StepCount should be reset by condensation\")\n\n\t// BaseCommit updated to new HEAD\n\tassert.Equal(t, newHead, newState.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"New ACTIVE session BaseCommit should be updated after condensation\")\n\n\t// Verify entire/checkpoints/v1 exists (new session was condensed)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err,\n\t\t\"entire/checkpoints/v1 should exist (new session was condensed)\")\n}"
}
```

```
    14→// Content-aware overlap detection for checkpoint management.
    15→//
    16→// These functions determine whether a commit contains session-related work by comparing
    17→// file content (not just filenames) against the shadow branch. This enables accurate
    18→// detection of the "reverted and replaced" scenario where a user:
    19→// 1. Reverts session changes (e.g., git checkout -- file.txt)
    20→// 2. Creates completely different content in the same file
    21→// 3. Commits the new content
    22→//
    23→// In this scenario, the commit should NOT get a checkpoint trailer because the
    24→// session's work was discarded, not incorporated.
    25→//
    26→// The key distinction:
    27→// - Modified files (exist in parent commit): Always count as overlap, regardless of
    28→//   content changes. The user is editing session's work.
    29→// - New files (don't exist in parent): Require content match against shadow branch.
    30→//   If content differs completely, the session's work was likely reverted & replaced.
    31→
    32→// filesOverlapWithContent checks if any file in filesTouched overlaps with the committed
    33→// content, using content-aware comparison to detect the "reverted and replaced" scenario.
    34→//
    35→// This is used in PostCommit to determine if a session has work in the commit.
    36→func filesOverlapWithContent(repo *git.Repository, shadowBranchName string, headCommit *object.Commit, filesTouched []string) bool {

    38→
    39→	// Build set of filesTouched for quick lookup

    41→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
    42→		touchedSet[f] = true
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Get HEAD commit tree (the committed content)
    46→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get HEAD tree, falling back to filename check",
    49→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    50→		)
    51→		return len(filesTouched) > 0 // Fall back: assume overlap if any files touched
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// Get shadow branch tree (the session's content)
    55→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
    56→	shadowRef, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: shadow branch not found, falling back to filename check",
    59→			slog.String("branch", shadowBranchName),
    60→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    61→		)
    62→		return len(filesTouched) > 0 // Fall back: assume overlap if any files touched
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get shadow commit, falling back to filename check",
    68→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    69→		)
    70→		return len(filesTouched) > 0
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	shadowTree, err := shadowCommit.Tree()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get shadow tree, falling back to filename check",
    76→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    77→		)
    78→		return len(filesTouched) > 0
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	// Get the parent commit tree to determine if files are modified vs newly created.
    82→	// For modified files (exist in parent), we count as overlap regardless of content
    83→	// because the user is editing the session's work.
    84→	// For newly created files (don't exist in parent), we check content to detect
    85→	// the "reverted and replaced" scenario where user deleted session's work and
    86→	// created something completely different.
    87→	var parentTree *object.Tree
    88→	if headCommit.NumParents() > 0 {
    89→		if parent, err := headCommit.Parent(0); err == nil {

    91→				parentTree = pTree

    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Check each file in filesTouched
    97→	for _, filePath := range filesTouched {
    98→		// Get file from HEAD tree (the committed content)
    99→		headFile, err := headTree.File(filePath)
   100→		if err != nil {
   101→			// File not in HEAD commit. This happens when:
   102→			// - The session created/modified the file but user deleted it before committing
   103→			// - The file was staged as a deletion (git rm)
   104→			// In both cases, the session's work on this file is not in the commit,
   105→			// so it doesn't contribute to overlap. Continue checking other files.
   106→			continue
   107→		}
   108→
   109→		// Check if this is a modified file (exists in parent) or new file
   110→		isModified := false
   111→		if parentTree != nil {
   112→			if _, err := parentTree.File(filePath); err == nil {
   113→				isModified = true
   114→			}
   115→		}
   116→
   117→		// Modified files always count as overlap (user edited session's work)

   155→}

   204→	}
   205→
   206→	// Build a map of index entries for O(1) lookup (avoid O(n*m) nested loop)
   207→	indexEntries := make(map[string]plumbing.Hash, len(idx.Entries))
   208→	for _, entry := range idx.Entries {
   209→		indexEntries[entry.Name] = entry.Hash

   317→
   318→// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.
   319→// This is a fallback when content-aware comparison isn't possible.
   320→func hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {
   321→	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
   322→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
   323→		touchedSet[f] = true
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	for _, staged := range stagedFiles {
   327→		if touchedSet[staged] {
   328→			return true
   329→		}
   330→	}
   331→	return false
   332→}
   333→
   334→// filesWithRemainingAgentChanges returns files from filesTouched that still have
   335→// uncommitted agent changes. This is used for carry-forward after partial commits.
   336→//
   337→// A file has remaining agent changes if:
   338→//   - It wasn't committed at all (not in committedFiles), OR
   339→//   - It was committed but the committed content doesn't match the shadow branch
   340→//     (user committed partial changes, e.g., via git add -p)
   341→//
   342→// Falls back to file-level subtraction if shadow branch is unavailable.
   343→func filesWithRemainingAgentChanges(
   344→	repo *git.Repository,
   345→	shadowBranchName string,
   346→	headCommit *object.Commit,
   347→	filesTouched []string,
   348→	committedFiles map[string]struct{},
   349→) []string {
   350→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   351→
   352→	// Get HEAD commit tree (the committed content)
   353→	commitTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   354→	if err != nil {
   355→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: failed to get commit tree, falling back to file subtraction",
   356→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   357→		)
   358→		return subtractFilesByName(filesTouched, committedFiles)
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	// Get shadow branch tree (the session's full content)
   362→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   363→	shadowRef, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   364→	if err != nil {
   365→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: shadow branch not found, falling back to file subtraction",
   366→			slog.String("branch", shadowBranchName),
   367→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   368→		)
   369→		return subtractFilesByName(filesTouched, committedFiles)
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   373→	if err != nil {
   374→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: failed to get shadow commit, falling back to file subtraction",
   375→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   376→		)
   377→		return subtractFilesByName(filesTouched, committedFiles)
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	shadowTree, err := shadowCommit.Tree()
   381→	if err != nil {
   382→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: failed to get shadow tree, falling back to file subtraction",
   383→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   384→		)
   385→		return subtractFilesByName(filesTouched, committedFiles)
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	var remaining []string
   389→
   390→	for _, filePath := range filesTouched {
   391→		// If file wasn't committed at all, it definitely has remaining changes
   392→		if _, wasCommitted := committedFiles[filePath]; !wasCommitted {
   393→			remaining = append(remaining, filePath)
   394→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: file not committed, keeping",
   395→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   396→			)
   397→			continue
   398→		}
   399→
   400→		// File was committed - check if committed content matches shadow branch
   401→		shadowFile, err := shadowTree.File(filePath)
   402→		if err != nil {
   403→			// File not in shadow branch - nothing to carry forward for this file
   404→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: file not in shadow branch, skipping",
   405→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   406→			)
   407→			continue
   408→		}
   409→
   410→		commitFile, err := commitTree.File(filePath)
   411→		if err != nil {
   412→			// File not in commit tree (deleted?) - keep it if it's in shadow
   413→			remaining = append(remaining, filePath)
   414→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: file not in commit tree but in shadow, keeping",
   415→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   416→			)
   417→			continue
   418→		}
   419→
   420→		// Compare hashes - if different, there are still uncommitted agent changes
   421→		if commitFile.Hash != shadowFile.Hash {
   422→			remaining = append(remaining, filePath)
   423→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: content mismatch, keeping for carry-forward",
   424→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   425→				slog.String("commit_hash", commitFile.Hash.String()[:7]),
   426→				slog.String("shadow_hash", shadowFile.Hash.String()[:7]),
   427→			)
   428→		} else {
   429→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: content fully committed",
   430→				slog.String("file", filePath),
   431→			)
   432→		}
   433→	}
   434→
   435→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesWithRemainingAgentChanges: result",
   436→		slog.Int("files_touched", len(filesTouched)),
   437→		slog.Int("committed_files", len(committedFiles)),
   438→		slog.Int("remaining_files", len(remaining)),
   439→	)
   440→
   441→	return remaining
   442→}
   443→
   444→// subtractFilesByName returns files from filesTouched that are NOT in committedFiles.
   445→// This is a fallback when content-aware comparison isn't possible.
   446→func subtractFilesByName(filesTouched []string, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) []string {
   447→	var remaining []string
   448→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
   449→		if _, committed := committedFiles[f]; !committed {
   450→			remaining = append(remaining, f)
   451→		}
   452→	}
   453→	return remaining
   454→}
   455→
   456→// hasSignificantContentOverlap checks if two file contents share significant lines.
   457→// This distinguishes partial staging (user kept some agent content) from
   458→// "reverted and replaced" (user wrote completely different content).
   459→//
   460→// For larger files, we require at least 2 matching lines because a single match
   461→// (like "package main") is likely common boilerplate. For small files (≤ 2
   462→// significant lines in either file), any single match counts as overlap since
   463→// there aren't many lines to match anyway.
   464→//
   465→// The function filters out trivial lines (short lines < 10 chars like braces,
   466→// empty lines, etc.) because these commonly appear in many files and don't
   467→// indicate meaningful overlap.
   468→func hasSignificantContentOverlap(stagedContent, shadowContent string) bool {
   469→	// Build set of significant lines from shadow (agent) content
   470→	shadowLines := extractSignificantLines(shadowContent)
   471→
   472→	// Build set of significant lines from staged (user) content
   473→	stagedLines := extractSignificantLines(stagedContent)
   474→
   475→	// If either has no significant lines, no meaningful overlap possible
   476→	if len(shadowLines) == 0 || len(stagedLines) == 0 {
   477→		return false
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	// For very small files (0-1 significant lines), any single match is meaningful
   481→	// since requiring 2 matches would be impossible. The 2-line requirement is
   482→	// mainly to filter out coincidental boilerplate (like "package main") in
   483→	// larger files where we CAN require multiple matches.
   484→	isVerySmallFile := len(shadowLines) < 2 || len(stagedLines) < 2
   485→	requiredMatches := 2
   486→	if isVerySmallFile {
   487→		requiredMatches = 1
   488→	}
   489→
   490→	// Count matching lines
   491→	matchCount := 0
   492→	for line := range stagedLines {
   493→		if shadowLines[line] {
   494→			matchCount++
   495→			if matchCount >= requiredMatches {
   496→				return true
   497→			}
   498→		}
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	return false
   502→}
   503→
   504→// extractSignificantLines returns a set of significant lines from content.
   505→// Lines are trimmed and must be at least 10 characters.
   506→//
   507→// This simple length-based filter works well because:
   508→// - Most structural boilerplate is short: `{`, `}`, `});`, `} else {` (all < 10 chars)
   509→// - Long lines (>= 10 chars) are usually meaningful regardless of language
   510→// - No language-specific pattern matching needed
   511→func extractSignificantLines(content string) map[string]bool {
   512→	lines := make(map[string]bool)
   513→	for _, line := range splitLines([]byte(content)) {
   514→		trimmed := trimLine(line)
   515→		if len(trimmed) >= 10 {
   516→			lines[trimmed] = true
   517→		}
   518→	}
   519→	return lines
   520→}
   521→
   522→// trimLine removes leading and trailing whitespace from a line.
   523→func trimLine(line string) string {
   524→	start := 0
   525→	end := len(line)
   526→	for start < end && (line[start] == ' ' || line[start] == '\t') {
   527→		start++
   528→	}
   529→	for end > start && (line[end-1] == ' ' || line[end-1] == '\t') {
   530→		end--
   531→	}
   532→	return line[start:end]
   533→}
   534→
```

I see the issue. `filesOverlapWithContent` considers any file that exists in the parent tree as "overlap" even if it wasn't actually changed in the commit. For stale sessions, `test.txt` exists in the tree but wasn't modified in this commit, yet it passes the overlap check.

I need to filter `filesTouchedBefore` to only include files actually changed in the commit before calling the overlap check.
