> AGENT

Now I can see exactly what happened. The log tells the whole story:

**Line 5585** — Session `37a27d31` (merge conflicts):
```
phase: "idle", has_new: true, should_condense: true
```

**Line 5621** — Session `7505c804` (logging plan):
```
HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched, phase: "ended", has_new: true, files_touched: 1
```

**Line 5632** — Session `76d407bd` (PR review):
```
HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched, phase: "ended", has_new: true, files_touched: 2
```

So sessions 3 and 4 were **ENDED**, not ACTIVE. They went through `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` which doesn't do overlap checking at all (line 530-536 of the current code). And they had `files_touched > 0` — meaning carry-forward had set their `FilesTouched` from a previous condensation.

Session 1 was **IDLE** with `has_new: true` — `sessionHasNewContent` returned true for it somehow.

The fix we just shipped only applies to `HandleCondense`. It doesn't touch `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched`, which is the path ENDED sessions take. And ENDED sessions with `FilesTouched` (from carry-forward) will keep getting condensed into every commit indefinitely.

This is a broader problem than stale ACTIVE sessions — it's also stale ENDED and IDLE sessions getting re-condensed due to carry-forward `FilesTouched` and `sessionHasNewContent` returning true on a shared shadow branch.

> DEVELOPER

so how do we actually fix this properly?

> AGENT

The logs tell the full story. Let me lay out what's actually happening:

**The sessions and their phases at condensation time:**

| Session | Phase | Handler | Why condensed? |
|---------|-------|---------|---------------|
| 0 (PR review) | **ENDED** | `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` | `files_touched=1, has_new=true` |
| 1 (merge conflicts) | **IDLE** | `HandleCondense` | `has_new=true` |
| 2 (PR review) | **IDLE** | `HandleCondense` | `has_new=true` |
| 3 (logging plan) | **ENDED** | `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` | `files_touched=1, has_new=true` |
| 4 (PR review) | **ENDED** | `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` | `files_touched=2, has_new=true` |
| 5 (PR review) | **ENDED** | `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` | (same pattern) |
| 6 (PR review) | **ACTIVE** | `HandleCondense` | `has_new=true` (the actual working session) |

So it's not stale ACTIVE sessions at all. It's mostly **ENDED sessions** going through `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched`, which has **zero overlap checking** — it just checks `len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew`.

**The self-perpetuating cycle:**

1. Session finishes (ENDED), gets condensed on first commit — carry-forward sets `FilesTouched` with uncommitted files
2. Next commit: `sessionHasNewContent` returns `true` because `FilesTouched` is non-empty (line 949-950) → `hasNew = true`
3. `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` sees `FilesTouched > 0 && hasNew` → condenses again
4. Carry-forward recalculates remaining files → sets `FilesTouched` again
5. Goto 2, forever

The PR we just shipped only fixes `HandleCondense` (IDLE/ACTIVE path). The ENDED path through `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` is completely unguarded. That's where most of these sessions went.

**The proper fix is to apply the same overlap check to `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched`:**

```go
func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {
    shouldCondense := len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew
    if shouldCondense && len(h.filesTouchedBefore) > 0 {
        var committedTouchedFiles []string
        for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
            if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
                committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
            }
        }
        if len(committedTouchedFiles) > 0 {
            shouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
        } else {
            shouldCondense = false
        }
    }
    // ...
}
```

This way ALL condensation paths — ACTIVE, IDLE, and ENDED — verify that committed files actually overlap with session files before condensing. The carry-forward cycle breaks because unrelated commits no longer trigger condensation.

Want me to implement this and update the PR?

> AGENT

The pattern is clear — it's been snowballing since **Feb 8**:

```
Feb 8:   1 → 1 → 2 → 2 sessions per checkpoint  (session 37a27d31 starts re-condensing)
Feb 9:   2 → 1 sessions
Feb 10:  2 → 1 → 1 → 1 sessions
Feb 18:  6 sessions  (8-day gap, big accumulation)
Feb 19:  7 sessions  (the one you noticed)
```

Session `37a27d31` (merge conflicts, Feb 8) was the first zombie — it started re-condensing on its second commit that same day. Each subsequent session that finished became another zombie via carry-forward. By Feb 18, six zombies were piling into every checkpoint, growing to seven by Feb 19.

The first condensation for each session is legitimate. After that, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched`, which makes `sessionHasNewContent` return true, which feeds `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` (ENDED) or `HandleCondense` (IDLE), and the cycle repeats on every commit. It's been running for **11 days**.

```
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")

	repo, err := OpenRepository()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
	head, err := repo.Head()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
	if !found {
		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
		)
		return nil
	}

	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
	if err != nil {
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
	}

	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
		)
		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
	}

	// Filter to sessions with new content
	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
	if len(sessionsWithContent) == 0 {
		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no new content to condense",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
			slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
		)
		// Still update BaseCommit for all sessions in this worktree
		// This prevents stale BaseCommit when commits happen without condensation
		// (e.g., when reusing a previous checkpoint ID for split commits)
		newHead := head.Hash().String()
		for _, state := range sessions {
			if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
				state.BaseCommit = newHead
				if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
					logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: failed to update session BaseCommit",
						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
						slog.String("error", err.Error()),
					)
				}
			}
		}
		return nil
	}

	// Track shadow branch names to clean up after successful condensation
	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})

	// Condense sessions that have new content
	for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
		// Compute shadow branch name BEFORE condensation modifies state.BaseCommit
		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)

		result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
		if err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
				state.SessionID, err)
			continue
		}

		// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
		shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}

		// Update session state for the new base commit
		// After condensation, the session continues from the NEW commit (HEAD), so we:
		// 1. Update BaseCommit to new HEAD - session now tracks from new commit
		// 2. Reset CheckpointCount to 0 - no checkpoints exist on new shadow branch yet
		// 3. Update CondensedTranscriptLines - track transcript position for detecting new content
		// 4. Clear PromptAttributions - they were already used in condensation, reset for next cycle
		//
		// This is critical: if we don't update BaseCommit, listAllSessionStates will try
		// to find shadow branch for old commit (which gets deleted), and since CheckpointCount > 0,
		// it will clean up (delete) the session state file. By updating to new HEAD with
		// CheckpointCount = 0, the session is preserved even without a shadow branch.
		state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
		state.CheckpointCount = 0
		state.CondensedTranscriptLines = result.TotalTranscriptLines

		// Clear attribution tracking - condensation already used these values
		// If we don't clear them, they'll be double-counted in the next condensation
		state.PromptAttributions = nil
		state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil

		// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits without new content can reuse it
		state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID

		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
		}

		shortID := state.SessionID
		if len(shortID) > 8 {
			shortID = shortID[:8]
		}
		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
			shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)

		// Log condensation
		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
		logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
			slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
			slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
			slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
		)
	}

	// Clean up shadow branches after successful condensation
	// Data is now preserved on entire/sessions, so shadow branches are no longer needed
	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
		} else {
			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)

			// Log branch cleanup
			logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
			)
		}
	}

	return nil
```

There it is. This is the pre-state-machine PostCommit from Feb 8. The key section:

```go
// Filter to sessions with new content
sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
```

Then it condenses ALL sessions in `sessionsWithContent` — **no overlap check at all**. Every session where `sessionHasNewContent` returned true got condensed, regardless of whether its files had anything to do with the commit.

> AGENT

The bug has been there since the initial commit. The original PostCommit:

1. Called `filterSessionsWithNewContent` → `sessionHasNewContent` for each session
2. Condensed ALL sessions where `sessionHasNewContent` returned true
3. **Never checked** whether the session's files overlapped with the committed files

The overlap check (`filesOverlapWithContent`) wasn't added until Feb 14, and even then it only applied to non-ACTIVE sessions in `HandleCondense`, never to ENDED sessions in `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched`.

**Answer: The bug has existed since the initial commit (`e72157d0`).** It just wasn't visible until multiple sessions accumulated in the same worktree — which started happening on Feb 8 when you had several sessions on the `alex/ent-207-fix-agent-name-misattribution` branch.

  "old_string": "func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {\n\t// Require that the committed files overlap with the session's files AND\n\t// have matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit would incorrectly\n\t// get this session's checkpoint.\n\t//\n\t// This applies to ALL sessions including ACTIVE ones. A stale ACTIVE session\n\t// (agent killed without Stop hook) would otherwise be condensed into every\n\t// commit because hasNew=true unconditionally for ACTIVE sessions.\n\t//\n\t// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:\n\t//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set by SaveChanges)\n\t//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions with empty FilesTouched\n\t//\n\t// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no\n\t// SaveChanges yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.\n\tshouldCondense := h.hasNew\n\tif shouldCondense && len(h.filesTouchedBefore) > 0 {\n\t\t// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.\n\t\t// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed\n\t\t// would pass the \"modified file\" check in filesOverlapWithContent\n\t\t// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale\n\t\t// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.\n\t\tvar committedTouchedFiles []string\n\t\tfor _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {\n\t\t\tif _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {\n\t\t\t\tcommittedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif len(committedTouchedFiles) > 0 {\n\t\t\tshouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tshouldCondense = false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif shouldCondense {\n\t\th.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)\n\t} else {\n\t\th.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {\n\tif len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.hasNew {\n\t\th.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)\n\t} else {\n\t\th.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {\n\tif h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() {\n\t\th.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)\n\t} else {\n\t\th.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched(state *session.State) error {\n\tif len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && h.shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() {\n\t\th.condensed = h.s.condenseAndUpdateState(h.logCtx, h.repo, h.checkpointID, state, h.head, h.shadowBranchName, h.shadowBranchesToDelete, h.committedFileSet)\n\t} else {\n\t\th.s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(h.logCtx, state, h.newHead)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck returns true if the session should be condensed\n// into this commit. Requires both that hasNew is true AND that the session's files\n// overlap with the committed files with matching content.\n//\n// This prevents stale sessions (ACTIVE sessions where the agent was killed, or\n// ENDED/IDLE sessions with carry-forward files) from being condensed into every\n// unrelated commit.\n//\n// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:\n//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set by SaveChanges)\n//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions with empty FilesTouched\n//\n// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no SaveChanges\n// yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.\nfunc (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() bool {\n\tif !h.hasNew {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {\n\t\treturn true // Fail-open: no file list to check against\n\t}\n\t// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.\n\t// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed\n\t// would pass the \"modified file\" check in filesOverlapWithContent\n\t// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale\n\t// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.\n\tvar committedTouchedFiles []string\n\tfor _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {\n\t\tif _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {\n\t\t\tcommittedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\treturn filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)\n}"
}

It doesn't test the case where `hasNew = true` but files don't overlap — which is the actual bug from the logs (ENDED sessions with carry-forward files and `hasNew = true`).

{
  "command": "gh pr edit 418 --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nFixes zombie sessions being re-condensed into every commit indefinitely.\n\n**Root cause:** After condensation, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched` with uncommitted files. On subsequent commits, `sessionHasNewContent` returns true (shadow branch has content), and the condensation handlers condense the session again without checking whether the committed files actually relate to the session. This cycle repeats on every commit.\n\n**Affected code paths:**\n- `HandleCondense` (ACTIVE/IDLE sessions) — skipped the overlap check entirely for ACTIVE sessions (`hasNew=true` unconditionally)\n- `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` (ENDED sessions) — had zero overlap checking, only checked `len(FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew`\n\n**Impact:** In one real case, 6 zombie sessions accumulated over 11 days (Feb 8–19), polluting 12 checkpoints with 18 duplicate condensation events.\n\n**Fix:** Extract `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` as a shared method that intersects session files with actually-committed files before calling `filesOverlapWithContent`. Both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` now use this method, ensuring ALL condensation paths verify file overlap.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` — ENDED session with carry-forward not condensed into unrelated commit\n- [x] `TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed` — stale ACTIVE session not condensed into unrelated commit\n- [x] `TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure` — updated to include `second.txt` in session's `FilesTouched`\n- [x] All existing PostCommit tests pass (ActiveSession, OldIdleSession, OldEndedSession, CarryForward, etc.)\n- [x] `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` passes\n\n🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Update PR description"
}
```

> AGENT

The script runs `git status --porcelain` which will always show `go.mod`/`go.sum` as modified during a merge because they differ from the pre-merge HEAD. This is expected — the lint check will pass after we complete the merge commit. Let me verify the other checks pass and run tests, then commit the merge.

"### IDLE sessions with empty FilesTouched bypass overlap check\n\n**Medium Severity**\n\n<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->\nThe guard condition changed from phase-based (`!state.Phase.IsActive()`) to file-count-based (`len(h.filesTouchedBefore) > 0`), which introduces a regression for IDLE sessions with empty `FilesTouched`. When `filesTouchedBefore` is empty for an IDLE session (no ACTIVE transcript fallback applies), the overlap check is entirely skipped, and `shouldCondense` stays as `hasNew`. Previously, `filesOverlapWithContent` would be called with an empty list and correctly return `false`. This means IDLE sessions with transcript growth but no file modifications (e.g., conversation-only sessions) can now get incorrectly condensed into unrelated commits — the same class of bug this PR aims to fix for ACTIVE sessions.\n<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->\n\n<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: 2a8a55d7-5801-4193-a06d-9c55c9f9f2ce -->\n\n<!-- LOCATIONS START\ncmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go#L503-L520\nLOCATIONS END -->\n<p><a href=\"https://cursor.com/open?REDACTED" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Cursor\" width=\"115\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"></picture></a>&nbsp;<a href=\"https://cursor.com/agents?REDACTED" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Web\" width=\"99\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"></picture></a></p>\n\n"

One review comment from Cursor Bugbot. Let me evaluate it.

**Bugbot finding:** `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` returns `true` (fail-open) when `filesTouchedBefore` is empty. For IDLE sessions with no `FilesTouched`, the old code would call `filesOverlapWithContent` with an empty list which returned `false` (not condensed). The new code fail-opens and condenses them — same class of bug we're fixing.

Let me verify this is a real issue by checking the code.

     1→package strategy

     3→import (
     4→	"context"

     8→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/logging"
     9→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5"
    10→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing"
    11→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"

    13→
    14→// Content-aware overlap detection for checkpoint management.
    15→//
    16→// These functions determine whether a commit contains session-related work by comparing
    17→// file content (not just filenames) against the shadow branch. This enables accurate
    18→// detection of the "reverted and replaced" scenario where a user:
    19→// 1. Reverts session changes (e.g., git checkout -- file.txt)
    20→// 2. Creates completely different content in the same file
    21→// 3. Commits the new content
    22→//
    23→// In this scenario, the commit should NOT get a checkpoint trailer because the
    24→// session's work was discarded, not incorporated.
    25→//
    26→// The key distinction:
    27→// - Modified files (exist in parent commit): Always count as overlap, regardless of
    28→//   content changes. The user is editing session's work.
    29→// - New files (don't exist in parent): Require content match against shadow branch.
    30→//   If content differs completely, the session's work was likely reverted & replaced.
```

   534→//
   535→// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:
   536→//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set by SaveChanges)
   537→//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions with empty FilesTouched
   538→//
   539→// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no SaveChanges
   540→// yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.
   541→func (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() bool {
   542→	if !h.hasNew {
   543→		return false
   544→	}
   545→	if len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {
   546→		return true // Fail-open: no file list to check against
   547→	}
   548→	// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.
   549→	// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed
   550→	// would pass the "modified file" check in filesOverlapWithContent
   551→	// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale
   552→	// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.
   553→	var committedTouchedFiles []string
   554→	for _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {
   555→		if _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ok {
   556→			committedTouchedFiles = append(committedTouchedFiles, f)
   557→		}
   558→	}
   559→	if len(committedTouchedFiles) == 0 {
   560→		return false
   561→	}
   562→	return filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, committedTouchedFiles)
   563→}
```

> AGENT

The bugbot is correct. The old code for IDLE sessions:
1. `shouldCondense = hasNew` → true
2. `!state.Phase.IsActive()` → true (IDLE is not ACTIVE)
3. Calls `filesOverlapWithContent(repo, shadowBranch, commit, state.FilesTouched)` with empty list → returns `false`
4. Result: **not condensed**

New code for IDLE sessions with empty `FilesTouched`:
1. `hasNew` → true
2. `len(filesTouchedBefore) == 0` → true (since `filesTouchedBefore = state.FilesTouched` for IDLE)
3. Fail-open returns **true**
4. Result: **condensed** (regression)

The fix: change the fail-open to `false`. If a session has touched zero files, there's no reason to condense it into any commit — the transcript stays on the shadow branch until a related commit happens.

    30→//   If content differs completely, the session's work was likely reverted & replaced.
    31→
    32→// filesOverlapWithContent checks if any file in filesTouched overlaps with the committed
    33→// content, using content-aware comparison to detect the "reverted and replaced" scenario.
    34→//
    35→// This is used in PostCommit to determine if a session has work in the commit.
    36→func filesOverlapWithContent(repo *git.Repository, shadowBranchName string, headCommit *object.Commit, filesTouched []string) bool {
    37→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
    38→
    39→	// Build set of filesTouched for quick lookup
    40→	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
    41→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
    42→		touchedSet[f] = true
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	// Get HEAD commit tree (the committed content)
    46→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
    47→	if err != nil {
    48→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get HEAD tree, falling back to filename check",
    49→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    50→		)
    51→		return len(filesTouched) > 0 // Fall back: assume overlap if any files touched
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	// Get shadow branch tree (the session's content)
    55→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
    56→	shadowRef, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
    57→	if err != nil {
    58→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: shadow branch not found, falling back to filename check",
    59→			slog.String("branch", shadowBranchName),
    60→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    61→		)
    62→		return len(filesTouched) > 0 // Fall back: assume overlap if any files touched
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get shadow commit, falling back to filename check",
    68→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    69→		)
    70→		return len(filesTouched) > 0
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	shadowTree, err := shadowCommit.Tree()
    74→	if err != nil {
    75→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "filesOverlapWithContent: failed to get shadow tree, falling back to filename check",
    76→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
    77→		)
    78→		return len(filesTouched) > 0
    79→	}
```

> AGENT

Confirmed — `filesOverlapWithContent` falls back to `len(filesTouched) > 0` in error cases, and when `filesTouched` is empty, that returns `false`. The bugbot's analysis is correct.

The old code path for an IDLE session with empty `FilesTouched`:
- `filesOverlapWithContent(repo, shadowBranch, commit, []string{})` → `false` → not condensed

New code path:
- `len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0` → `return true` (fail-open) → condensed

This is a valid regression. The fix is straightforward — change the fail-open to `false`. If a session has touched zero files, there's nothing to condense into any commit. The transcript stays safely on the shadow branch until a related commit happens.

  "old_string": "// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no SaveChanges\n// yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.\nfunc (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() bool {\n\tif !h.hasNew {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {\n\t\treturn true // Fail-open: no file list to check against\n\t}",
  "new_string": "// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (no files touched yet), we return false\n// because there are no files to overlap with the commit.\nfunc (h *postCommitActionHandler) shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck() bool {\n\tif !h.hasNew {\n\t\treturn false\n\t}\n\tif len(h.filesTouchedBefore) == 0 {\n\t\treturn false // No files touched = no overlap possible\n\t}"
}

  1640→// commit from a different session.
  1641→//
  1642→// Root cause: when an agent is killed without the Stop hook firing, its session
  1643→// remains in ACTIVE phase permanently. Previously, PostCommit unconditionally
  1644→// set hasNew=true for ACTIVE sessions and skipped the filesOverlapWithContent
  1645→// check, so stale ACTIVE sessions got condensed into every commit.
  1646→//
  1647→// The fix applies the overlap check to ALL sessions (including ACTIVE) using
  1648→// filesTouchedBefore, so stale sessions with unrelated files are filtered out.
  1649→func TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed(t *testing.T) {

   656→		//   - No TTY (agent/subagent committing) — added unconditionally
   657→		//   - TTY (human committing) — added after content detection confirmed agent work
   658→		// In both cases, PrepareCommitMsg already validated this commit. We trust
   659→		// that decision here. Transcript-based re-validation is unreliable because
   660→		// subagent transcripts may not be available yet (subagent still running).

   668→				hasNew = true

Now I understand. For the IDLE session in my test, the shadow branch exists (from `setupSessionWithCheckpoint`). The shadow branch has a transcript on it. `sessionHasNewContent` will compare transcript lines on the shadow branch vs `CheckpointTranscriptStart`. Since I set `CheckpointTranscriptStart=0`, it should find new content.

But wait — the BaseCommit assertion is what failed, not the condensation check. The BaseCommit didn't get updated. Let me check `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` — maybe the issue is that the IDLE session's shadow branch is at the old base commit, so there's nothing to update?

Actually, the issue is simpler. Look at the test: `setupSessionWithCheckpoint` creates the session at the initial commit. Then we make a new commit. But the session's `BaseCommit` still points to the original commit. `updateBaseCommitIfChanged` only updates if `newHead != state.BaseCommit`, which it does differ... > AGENT

I see the issue. This test has an ACTIVE session with `StepCount=0` and no `FilesTouched`. With our fix, `filesTouchedBefore` is empty for this ACTIVE session, so `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck` returns `false`, and the session is NOT condensed.

The question is: should an ACTIVE session with no files touched still condense? The old behavior was yes (ACTIVE always condenses). But this is exactly the behavior that caused the stale session bug.

The difference is: a _legitimately_ active session (agent is currently running) vs a _stale_ active session (agent was killed). Both have `Phase=ACTIVE`. The question is whether we can distinguish them.

For a legitimately ACTIVE session making a mid-turn commit, the agent must have been doing _something_ to trigger the commit. If it hasn't touched any files, why would there be a commit? The transcript extraction fallback (`extractFilesFromLiveTranscript`) handles the case where `FilesTouched` is empty but the transcript shows files.

But there's an edge case: the ACTIVE session might have triggered a commit via tool use before any file modifications are saved to state. In that case, both `FilesTouched` and transcript extraction could be empty.

The right fix is more nuanced — only fail-open for ACTIVE sessions (preserving the original behavior), while returning `false` for IDLE/ENDED sessions with empty files. Let me thread the phase through.

> TOOL

Both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` receive `state *session.State`, which has the phase. But `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck` doesn't take the state. The cleanest approach: pass `isActive` to the method.

> AGENT

All clean. Now to your point about multi-session:

You're right. In a multi-session scenario, session A (ACTIVE) may not have touched any files, but session B (also running in the same directory) triggers a commit via its tool use. The commit has a checkpoint trailer from session B's `PrepareCommitMsg`. Session A has `hasNew=true` (it's ACTIVE) but empty `filesTouchedBefore` because it hasn't saved any checkpoints yet.

That's exactly the scenario in `TestPostCommit_ActiveSessionAlwaysCondenses` — an ACTIVE session with `StepCount=0` and no files. The fail-open for ACTIVE sessions preserves that behavior: the session gets condensed because `PrepareCommitMsg` already validated the trailer, and we trust that decision for ACTIVE sessions.

For stale ACTIVE sessions, the key difference is they DO have `filesTouchedBefore` populated (from their old `FilesTouched` or transcript), but those files don't overlap with the commit — so the intersection check catches them.

The final logic is:
- **ACTIVE + empty filesTouchedBefore**: fail-open (trust `hasNew`) — handles multi-session mid-turn commits
- **ACTIVE + non-empty filesTouchedBefore**: overlap check — catches stale sessions
- **IDLE/ENDED + empty filesTouchedBefore**: `false` — no files = no overlap
- **IDLE/ENDED + non-empty filesTouchedBefore**: overlap check — catches carry-forward zombies

Want me to commit this, push, and reply to the bugbot thread?

> AGENT

Interesting — `cmd/entire/cli` passes in **3.8s** on CI but hangs locally. The test works fine on CI (Linux) but hangs on macOS. This is a platform-specific issue — likely related to go-git's tree diff behavior differing between environments, or possibly our local repo having different git state (packed refs, more objects, etc.) that triggers the infinite loop.

This is a pre-existing local-only issue, not something our PR introduced. CI will pass.

> AGENT

Yep — it calls `runExplain` directly with no test repo setup. That means it uses whatever repo the test is running in — **our repo**. On CI that's a fresh shallow clone. Locally, it's our full repo with all its history, shadow branches, and checkpoint objects.

The `hasCodeChanges` call does `Tree.Diff` on every temporary checkpoint it finds, and our local repo has a ton of shadow branch commits with large trees. That's what causes the infinite loop / extreme slowness locally.

So it's not a bug per se — it's a test that assumes a small repo but doesn't create its own. It works on CI's shallow clone but hangs on a real working repo. Not our problem for this PR.

> DEVELOPER

this is a problem more generally then??

> AGENT

Yes — if `hasCodeChanges` hangs in the test because it's running against a real repo, it'll hang for users running `entire explain` on their actual repos too. The test is just surfacing the real problem.

> AGENT

`parentTree.Diff(commitTree)` — this is doing a full tree diff of the entire repo tree for every shadow branch checkpoint. On a repo with thousands of files, go-git's merkletrie diff is extremely slow (it walks every node). And this is called for every temporary checkpoint found.

This will hang `entire explain` for any user with a non-trivial repo. Want me to file an issue for this?

> DEVELOPER

yes please

> TOOL

tool_use mcp__linear-server__create_issue
id: toolu_01NaDkUubb7pnm48tVA8wgwY
```json
{
  "title": "`entire explain` hangs on non-trivial repos due to full tree diff in hasCodeChanges",
  "team": "Entire",
  "priority": 2,
  "description": "## Bug\n\n`entire explain` hangs indefinitely on repos with non-trivial history. The root cause is `hasCodeChanges()` in `explain.go:1568` which calls `parentTree.Diff(commitTree)` — a full go-git merkletrie tree diff — for every temporary checkpoint it encounters.\n\nOn large repos, this tree diff is extremely slow (walks every node in both trees). Since it's called for every checkpoint in `convertTemporaryCheckpoint` → `getReachableTemporaryCheckpoints` → `getBranchCheckpoints`, the explain command becomes unusable.\n\n## Reproduction\n\n```bash\n# In any repo with substantial history and shadow branches\nentire explain\n# Hangs indefinitely\n```\n\nThe test `TestRunExplain_SessionFlagFiltersListView` also hangs locally for the same reason — it calls `runExplain` against the actual working repo rather than a test fixture. It only passes in CI because CI uses a fresh shallow clone.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n`explain.go:1568`:\n```go\nchanges, err := parentTree.Diff(commitTree)\n```\n\ngo-git's `Tree.Diff()` does a full merkletrie walk of both trees. For shadow branch commits where the tree contains the entire repo snapshot, this is O(all files in repo) per checkpoint.\n\n## Suggested Fix\n\nOptions:\n1. **Use `git diff-tree` CLI** instead of go-git's `Tree.Diff()` — the git CLI is orders of magnitude faster for this\n2. **Compare tree hashes directly** — if parent and commit have different root tree hashes, check only the changed paths (git's native diff-tree does this efficiently)\n3. **Use `commit.Stats()` or `commit.Patch()`** with a context timeout so it can't hang indefinitely\n\n## Introduced\n\n- `hasCodeChanges` added in `b32b54f4` (Jan 27)\n- Hanging test added in `3e037829` (Feb 4)",
  "labels": [
    "Bug"
  ]
}
```

{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/entireio/cli/pulls/comments/2825783321","pull_request_review_id":3823604723,"id":2825783321,"node_id":"PRRC_kwDOQyo98M6obggZ","diff_hunk":"@@ -479,20 +479,45 @@ type postCommitActionHandler struct {\n \tshadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{}\n \tcommittedFileSet       map[string]struct{}\n \thasNew                 bool\n+\tfilesTouchedBefore     []string\n \n \t// Output: set by handler methods, read by caller after TransitionAndLog.\n \tcondensed bool\n }\n \n func (h *postCommitActionHandler) HandleCondense(state *session.State) error {\n-\t// For ACTIVE sessions, any commit during the turn is session-related.\n-\t// For IDLE/ENDED sessions (e.g., carry-forward), also require that the\n-\t// committed files overlap with the session's remaining files AND have\n-\t// matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit (or a commit with\n-\t// completely replaced content) would incorrectly get this session's checkpoint.\n+\t// Require that the committed files overlap with the session's files AND\n+\t// have matching content — otherwise an unrelated commit would incorrectly\n+\t// get this session's checkpoint.\n+\t//\n+\t// This applies to ALL sessions including ACTIVE ones. A stale ACTIVE session\n+\t// (agent killed without Stop hook) would otherwise be condensed into every\n+\t// commit because hasNew=true unconditionally for ACTIVE sessions.\n+\t//\n+\t// filesTouchedBefore is populated from:\n+\t//   - state.FilesTouched for IDLE/ENDED sessions (set by SaveChanges)\n+\t//   - transcript extraction for ACTIVE sessions with empty FilesTouched\n+\t//\n+\t// When filesTouchedBefore is empty (transcript extraction failed + no\n+\t// SaveChanges yet), we trust hasNew to avoid data loss for mid-turn commits.\n \tshouldCondense := h.hasNew\n-\tif shouldCondense && !state.Phase.IsActive() {\n-\t\tshouldCondense = filesOverlapWithContent(h.repo, h.shadowBranchName, h.commit, state.FilesTouched)\n+\tif shouldCondense && len(h.filesTouchedBefore) > 0 {\n+\t\t// Only check files that were actually changed in this commit.\n+\t\t// Without this, files that exist in the tree but weren't changed\n+\t\t// would pass the \"modified file\" check in filesOverlapWithContent\n+\t\t// (because the file exists in the parent tree), causing stale\n+\t\t// sessions to be incorrectly condensed.\n+\t\tvar committedTouchedFiles []string\n+\t\tfor _, f := range h.filesTouchedBefore {\n+\t\t\tif _, ok := h.committedFileSet[f]; ```
## Summary

Fixes zombie sessions being re-condensed into every commit indefinitely.

**Root cause:** After condensation, carry-forward sets `FilesTouched` with uncommitted files. On subsequent commits, `sessionHasNewContent` returns true (shadow branch has content), and the condensation handlers condense the session again without checking whether the committed files actually relate to the session. This cycle repeats on every commit.

**Affected code paths:**
- `HandleCondense` (ACTIVE/IDLE sessions) — skipped the overlap check entirely for ACTIVE sessions (`hasNew=true` unconditionally)
- `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` (ENDED sessions) — had zero overlap checking, only checked `len(FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew`

**Impact:** In one real case, 6 zombie sessions accumulated over 11 days (Feb 8–19), polluting 12 checkpoints with 18 duplicate condensation events.

**Fix:** Extract `shouldCondenseWithOverlapCheck()` as a shared method that intersects session files with actually-committed files before calling `filesOverlapWithContent`. Both `HandleCondense` and `HandleCondenseIfFilesTouched` now use this method, ensuring ALL condensation paths verify file overlap.

## Test plan

- [x] `TestPostCommit_EndedSessionCarryForward_NotCondensedIntoUnrelatedCommit` — ENDED session with carry-forward not condensed into unrelated commit
- [x] `TestPostCommit_StaleActiveSession_NotCondensed` — stale ACTIVE session not condensed into unrelated commit
- [x] `TestHandleTurnEnd_PartialFailure` — updated to include `second.txt` in session's `FilesTouched`
- [x] All existing PostCommit tests pass (ActiveSession, OldIdleSession, OldEndedSession, CarryForward, etc.)
- [x] `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci` passes

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
```
